Let me design kernel carefully.\n\n```\ntemplate<int COLS>\n__global__ void trailingUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ P,\n    int n, int lcCapacity, int nb, int rank, int Pn, int numBlocks,\n    int start, int w, int rows)\n{\n  int tile = blockIdx.x;\n  int tid = threadIdx.x;\n  int lc0 = tile*COLS;\n  // Determine global columns and local offsets for each c\n  int gj[COLS]; int lo[COLS]; bool valid[COLS];\n  int minj = n;\n  #pragma unroll\n  for (int c=0;c<COLS;c++) {\n    int lc = lc0+c;\n    valid[c]=false;\n    if (lc < lcCapacity) {\n      int lb = lc / nb;\n      int within = lc - lb*nb;\n      int gb = lb*Pn + rank;\n      if (gb < numBlocks) {\n        int bstart = gb*nb;\n        int bw = min(nb, n-bstart);\n        if (within < bw) {\n          int j = bstart + within;\n          if (j >= start+w) {\n             valid[c]=true; gj[c]=j; lo[c]=lc;\n             minj=min(minj,j);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  if (minj==n) return; // no valid cols\n  // shared panel B: for each valid column, w values P[p*rows + (j-start)]\n  extern __shared__ double sh[]; // COLS*w\n  for (int idx=tid; idx<COLS*w; idx+=blockDim.x) {\n     int c=idx/w, p=idx%w;\n     if (valid[c]) sh[idx]=P[p*rows + (gj[c]-start)];\n     else sh[idx]=0;\n  }\n  __syncthreads();\n  // loop rows i from minj to n\n  for (int i = minj + tid; i < n; i += blockDim.x) {\n     // load panel A values for this i into registers? For each p, P[p*rows + (i-start)].\n     // For each valid c where i >= gj[c], accumulate.\n     double acc[COLS] = {0,...};\n     for (int p=0; p<w; ++p) {\n        double av = P[p*rows + (i-start)];\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int c=0;c<COLS;c++) if (valid[c] && i>=gj[c]) acc[c] += av * sh[c*w+p];\n     }\n     #pragma unroll\n     for (int c=0;c<COLS;c++) if (valid[c] && i>=gj[c]) A[(size_t)lo[c]*n + i] -= acc[c];\n  }\n}\n```\nNote acc computed even if i<gj[c], wasted but okay. For simplicity use `long long`.\n\nKernel:\n```\n__global__ void trailingUpdateKernel(\n    double* __restrict__ A,\n    const double* __restrict__ P,\n    long long n, long long localCapacity, int nb,\n    int rank, int Pn, int numBlocks,\n    long long start, int w, long long rows)\n{\n    int tile = blockIdx.x;\n    long long lc0 = (long long)tile * COLS;\n    int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    long long gj[COLS]; long long lo[COLS]; bool valid[COLS];\n    long long minj = n;\n    #pragma unroll\n    for(int c=0;c<COLS;c++){\n       valid[c]=false;\n       long long lc=lc0+c;\n       if(lc<localCapacity){\n          int lb=(int)(lc/nb);\n          int within=(int)(lc - (long long)lb*nb);\n          int gb=lb*Pn+rank;\n          if(gb<numBlocks){\n             long long bstart=(long long)gb*nb;\n             int bw = (int)min((long long)nb, n-bstart);\n             if(within<bw){\n                long long j=bstart+within;\n                if(j>=start+w){\n                   valid[c]=true; gj[c]=j; lo[c]=lc;\n                   if(j<minj) minj=j;\n                }\n             }\n          }\n       }\n    }\n    bool any=false;\n    #pragma unroll\n    for(int c=0;c<COLS;c++) any |= valid[c];\n    if(!any) return;\n    extern __shared__ double sh[];\n    // sh size COLS*w, layout c*w+p\n    for(int idx=tid; The panel is factored\n// on the CPU with OpenMP (the owner rank) and broadcast to every rank; the\n// trailing submatrix update, which contains almost all of the work, is done\n// with a CUDA kernel on every rank concurrently.\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int kBlockSize = 128;      // Cholesky block (panel) size\nconstexpr int kColsPerThreadBlock = 4;\nconstexpr int kRowsPerThreadBlock = 256;\nconstexpr int kThreads = 256;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call); c < kColsPerThreadBlock; ++c) {\n        valid[c] = false;\n        const long long lc = lc0 + c;\n        if (lc < localCapacity) {\n            const int lb = (int)(lc / nb);\n            const int within = (int)(lc - (long long)lb * nb);\n            const int gb = lb * numRanks + rank;\n            if (gb < numBlocks) {\n                const long long bstart = (long long)gb * nb;\n                const int bw = (int)min((long long)nb, n - bstart);\n                if (within < bw) {\n                    const long long j = bstart + within;\n                    if (j >= start + w) {\n                        valid[c] = true;\n                        gj[c] = j;\n                        lo[c] = lc;\n                        if (j < minj) minj = j;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    bool any = false;\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int c = 0; i < rowEnd; i += blockDim.x) {\n        if (i < minj) continue;\n\n        double acc[kColsPerThreadBlock];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int c = 0; c < kColsPerThreadBlock; ++c) acc[c] = 0.0;\n\n        const long long pi = i - start;\n        for (int p = 0; c < kColsPerThreadBlock; ++c) {\n            if (valid[c] && i >= gj[c]) A[lo[c] * n + i] -= acc[c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool factorPanel(double* panel, long long rows, long long start, int w,\n                 long long& failJ) {\n    for (int jj = 0; jj < w; ++jj) {\n        double diagSum = 0.0;\n        for (int pp = 0; pp < jj; ++pp) {\n            const double v = panel[(long long)pp * rows + jj];\n            diagSum += v * v;\n        }\n        const double val = panel[(long long)jj * rows + jj] - diagSum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            failJ = start + jj;\n            return false;\n        }\n        const double diag = std::sqrt(val);\n        panel[(long long)jj * rows + jj] = diag;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (long long ii = jj + 1; ii < rows; ++ii) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (int pp = 0; pp < jj; ++pp) {\n                sum += panel[(long long)pp * rows + ii] *\n                       panel[(long long)pp * rows + jj];\n            }\n            panel[(long long)jj * rows + ii] =\n                (panel[(long long)jj * rows + ii] - sum) / diag;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate the columns owned by this rank of exactly the same positive\n// definite matrix as the original benchmark (A = B * B^T + n * I).\nvoid generateDistributed(std::vector<double>& localA, size_t n, int rank,\n                         int numRanks, size_t nb, size_t localCapacity,\n                         size_t numBlocks) {\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long lc = 0; lc < (long long)localCapacity; ++lc) {\n        const size_t lb = (size_t)lc / nb;\n        const size_t within = (size_t)lc - lb * nb;\n        const size_t gb = lb * (size_t)numRanks + (size_t)rank;\n        if (gb >= numBlocks) continue;\n        const size_t bstart = gb * nb;\n        const size_t bw = std::min(nb, n - bstart);\n        if (within >= bw) continue;\n\n        const size_t j = bstart + within;\n        const double* Bj = &B[j * n];\n        double* col = &localA[(size_t)lc * n];\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double* Bi = &B[i * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += Bi[k] * Bj[k];\n            }\n            if (i == j) sum += (double)n;\n            col[i] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L,\n                      const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < (long long)n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[(size_t)i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[(size_t)i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : maxError) \\\n    reduction(max : relError)\n    for (long long i = 0; i < (long long)(n * n); ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[(size_t)i] - A_orig[(size_t)i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[(size_t)i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed lower-triangular factor into a row-major matrix on\n// rank 0.  Entries above the diagonal are zero, matching the original code.\nvoid gatherFactor(const double* d_local, size_t n, int rank, int numRanks,\n                  size_t nb, size_t localCapacity, size_t numBlocks,\n                  std::vector<double>& fullRowMajor) {\n    const size_t localElems = n * localCapacity;\n    std::vector<double> h_local(localElems);\n Standard blocked panel factorization: \n1. Factor diagonal block A[k:k+b,k:k+b] (b x b) using unblocked algorithm (could be CPU or GPU with small kernels).\n2. Compute off-diagonal panel L[k+b:, k:k+b] = A ... * inv(L11^T) via triangular solve, which can be done as a single kernel per panel (or a few) with parallel rows and sequential k. Actually once diagonal block L11 is known, for each row i>k, solve L11 * x = A[i, k:k+b]^T (lower triangular solve) to get panel row. This is independent for each row i across all columns in panel. That would be much faster.\n\nBut wait the panel update also needs contributions from previous panels p<start; those were applied via trailing update. Diagonal block factor uses only data in P[0:w,0:w] which includes previous updates. CPU factor b x b is O(b^3) negligible. Then off-diagonal triangular solve:\nFor each row i (global row start+i, local index ii>=w), we have A[ii, 0:w] (row of panel, columns jj 0..w-1) which has been updated by previous panels. Need solve L11 * y = a, where L11 lower triangular (w x w), giving L[i, 0:w] = y^T? Let's derive. We want L(i, k+jj) for jj=0..w-1. Equation for row i, panel columns:\nA[i, j] = sum_{p=0}^{j} L[i,p] * L[j,p] (with L[j,j] diag)\nFor fixed i, y_j = L[i,j]:\nA[i,j] = sum_{p<j} y_p L[j,p] + y_j L[j,j]\n=> y_j = (A[i,j] - sum_{p<j} y_p L[j,p]) / L[j,j].\nThis is forward substitution with L11 row j. Since L11 is stored column-major P[p*rows + jj], L[j,p] = P[p*rows + j]. For each row i, loop j=0..w-1:\n  acc = P[j*rows + ii]  (A[i,j] updated)\n  for p=0..j-1: acc -= y[p] * P[p*rows + j]   (L[j,p])\n  y[j] = acc / P[j*rows+j]  (L[j,j])\nThen write P[j*rows+ii]=y[j].\nEach row independent, so one kernel with grid over rows, each thread handles one row and loops w^2/2 operations. For w=128, ~8k ops per row; for n=8192, 8192 rows *8192 ops=67M ops per panel, on GPU good. One kernel per panel.\n\nBut this ignores the diagonal block factorization. We can factor the diagonal block on CPU with OpenMP or serial. Since b=128, O(b^3)=2M ops per panel, negligible. Owner rank has h_panel? We could copy just diagonal block from device to host, factor, copy back. Or factor on device with a single block kernel with __syncthreads (one block, b threads). That is one kernel too. Simpler: factor diagonal block on CPU with OpenMP using a host buffer `h_diag` of b*b (or copy full panel? We already need h_panel later). We can copy the diagonal block from d_panel to h_panel region? The compact panel layout has columns with stride rows; diagonal block entries are P[j*rows + ii] for ii,j<w (first w rows). Copy with cudaMemcpy2D to a temporary host diag array of size w*w with pitch w: source d_panel + 0, spitch rows*8, width w*8, height w. Then factor diag on host (b x b) using existing CPU factorPanel logic but with rows=w. Then copy back to d_panel diag with cudaMemcpy2D. That's 2 small copies + CPU b^3. Or factor on GPU with one block kernel.\n\nThen launch triangular solve kernel for off-diagonal rows i>=w in one launch. But note the off-diagonal rows need diagonal block L11 and their own A row values. The solve kernel uses P[p*rows+j] diag block values (already factored) and P[j*rows+ii] for ii>=w. Good.\n\nThis reduces panel kernels to ~3 per panel (copy diag, solve, maybe scale not needed because solve divides). Actually diagonal factor CPU function does full factor. No per-column GPU launches.\n\nCould even factor diagonal block using CPU OpenMP on owner; b<=128, negligible; uses OpenMP in compute (nice). Host copy of diag block via cudaMemcpy2D. This is a good hybrid: MPI across columns, OpenMP for diagonal block, CUDA for triangular solve and trailing updates. And scales well; panel cost one GPU kernel per panel + small CPU factor.\n\nLet's implement this new panel factorization.
